- [[mitchellh-tripwire#^f1]] [zig/test] [zig/errors] errdefer cleanup is called one of the most error-prone parts of Zig programs, because error paths rarely run in tests. (B/strong, verified)
- [[mitchellh-tripwire#^f2]] [zig/test] defer is tested by any test, errdefer only if the error can be triggered; failing_allocator works only for allocation and is fragile in complex code. (B/strong, verified)
- [[mitchellh-tripwire#^f3]] [zig/test] Tripwire: named fail points (an enum) checked before fallible calls; the module's error set comes from the function itself. (B/strong, verified)
- [[mitchellh-tripwire#^f4]] [zig/test] testing.allocator turns an injected error into a leak check: a missing errdefer fails the test. (B/strong, verified)
- [[mitchellh-tripwire#^f5]] [zig/test] Loop over `std.meta.tags(FailPoint)` to cover every fail point. (B/strong, verified)
- [[mitchellh-tripwire#^f6]] [zig/test] `errorAfter` trips only after N hits (loop cleanup); `end` checks each point actually tripped. (B/strong, verified)
- [[mitchellh-tripwire#^f10]] [zig/test] He plans errdefer testing for all new Ghostty code. (B/strong, verified)
- [[mitchellh-zig-comptime-conditional-disable#^f7]] [zig/test] [zig/build] He builds both sides of every comptime switch in CI because the failure mode is silent. (B/strong, verified)
- [[river-repo#^f24]] [zig/test] Tests: only the reusable common/slotmap.zig has unit tests (3 test blocks in 15k lines), run by `zig build test`; the compositor itself has none. `build.zig`, `common/slotmap.zig` (A/strong, verified)
- [[roc-zig-compiler#^f5]] [zig/test] [zig/safety] Death tests pin that an invariant fires: the test re-runs itself in a child copy of the test binary and passes when the child panics with the expected message, because a panic ends the whole test process. `src/base/death_test.zig` (B/strong, verified)
- [[roc-zig-compiler#^f6]] [zig/test] [zig/build] Zig collects tests lazily: `refAllDecls(@This())` is one level and pub-only, an unreferenced import adds nothing, so a test file can silently never run. Roc checks every `test` decl appears in some test binary's metadata list. `ci/check_test_wiring.zig` (B/strong, verified)
- [[roc-zig-compiler#^f16]] [zig/test] Two test kinds: Zig unit tests next to the code with `test` for low-level details, and more commonly markdown snapshot files showing each compiler stage's output for a snippet, regenerated by a tool and diffed in CI. `AGENT.md` (B/strong, verified)
- [[roc-zig-compiler#^f17]] [zig/test] [zig/build] Tests run through custom build steps (`zig build run-test-zig -- --test-filter`), not `zig test`; a `zig build -Dno-bin -fincremental --watch` loop type-checks without emitting binaries for fast feedback. `AGENT.md`, `src/README.md` (B/strong, verified)
- [[seguin-custom-test-runner#^f1]] [zig/test] A test runner is just a `main` that iterates `builtin.test_functions`; set it with `.test_runner` on `addTest`. (B/fair, verified)
- [[seguin-custom-test-runner#^f2]] [zig/test] To keep leak detection, reset `std.testing.allocator_instance` before each test and check `deinit() == .leak` after. (B/fair, verified)
- [[seguin-custom-test-runner#^f3]] [zig/test] Test names encode the file path (`server.handshake.test.Handshake: parse`), which a runner can parse. (B/fair, verified)
- [[seguin-custom-test-runner#^f4]] [zig/test] Global setup/teardown via naming convention (`tests:beforeAll`) is a hack he finds useful. (B/fair, verified)
- [[seguin-generics-test-stubs#^f1]] [zig/test] Wrap the type as `ClientT(comptime S: type)` and export `pub const Client = ClientT(net.Stream)`; tests instantiate it with a recording stub. (B/fair, verified)
- [[seguin-generics-test-stubs#^f2]] [zig/test] The stub records writes into an ArrayList on `testing.allocator` so leaks are caught. (B/fair, verified)
- [[seguin-generics-test-stubs#^f3]] [zig/test] His testing stance: fast, not flaky, not brittle; stubs only in specific cases. (B/fair, verified)
- [[seguin-generics-test-stubs#^f4]] [zig/test] Alternative he names: pull the framing logic into its own struct and test it directly. (B/fair, verified)
- [[seguin-learning-zig-coding-in-zig#^f12]] [zig/test] Test-data factory: `anytype` plus `@hasField` lets a test set only the fields it cares about. (B/fair, verified)
- [[seguin-learning-zig-coding-in-zig#^f14]] [zig/test] He says `refAllDecls(@This())` in a `test {}` block pulls in nested containers' tests. (B/fair, verified)
- [[seguin-learning-zig-coding-in-zig#^f15]] [zig/test] (inferred) Limit on ^f14: `refAllDecls` only references the container's direct declarations; files reached only through nested decls need their own reference. - [[seguin-learning-zig-coding-in-zig#^f16]] [zig/test] The test runner is silent on success; `--summary all` shows counts. (B/fair, verified)
- [[seguin-learning-zig-heap-memory#^f10]] [zig/test] `std.testing.allocator` fails a test on leaks and prints the allocation's stack trace. (B/fair, verified)
- [[seguin-new-io-unsafe#^f3]] [zig/test] The failure depends on input, so small tests can pass. (C/fair, verified)
- [[superhtml-repo#^f21]] [zig/test] [toolchain] Structured fuzzing with the 0.17 `std.testing.fuzz` and `std.testing.Smith`: the fuzzer picks ops from an enum to build plausible HTML, then checks parse and render do not crash; a companion test replays a base64 case. `src/fuzz.zig` (B/strong, verified)
- [[superhtml-repo#^f22]] [zig/test] Also AFL fuzzing behind `-Dfuzz`, with the afl_kit dependency imported lazily so normal builds never fetch it. `build.zig` (B/strong, verified)
- [[superhtml-repo#^f23]] [zig/test] Formatter tests are round trips: input text equals its formatted output via `std.testing.expectFmt`, tests inline at the end of Ast.zig (26 there). `src/html/Ast.zig` (B/strong, verified)
- [[tigerbeetle-architecture#^f7]] [zig/test] Determinism is the meta principle above static allocation: same input, same logical result by the same physical path; any randomised test failure reproduces from its seed. (A/strong, verified)
- [[tigerbeetle-architecture#^f8]] [zig/test] Simulation testing is the most important technique: the whole cluster on one thread with storage faults and fast time; unlike TLA+ it tests the implementation. (A/strong, verified)
- [[tigerbeetle-architecture#^f13]] [zig/test] VOPR stubs every non-deterministic part (clock, network, disk), injects faults from a seed, and replays any failure from seed plus commit; one VOPR minute equals days of real testing. `docs/internals/vopr.md` (A/strong, verified)
- [[tigerbeetle-blog-four-fuzzers#^f1]] [zig/test] Besides the whole-system simulator, every subsystem gets its own targeted fuzzer: the big one is slow to reach deep layers and can't judge quality. (A/strong, verified)
- [[tigerbeetle-blog-four-fuzzers#^f2]] [zig/test] [zig/api] Fuzzable design starts with minimal interfaces sketched before code; mechanical extraction ossifies accidental dependencies. (A/strong, verified)
- [[tigerbeetle-blog-four-fuzzers#^f3]] [zig/test] Small spaces: drive the same random test with an exhaustive generator to cover every case. (A/strong, verified)
- [[tigerbeetle-blog-four-fuzzers#^f4]] [zig/test] Test negative space too: decode random bytes then re-encode (`serialize . deserialize`), and crash on invalid internal data (offensive programming: `.?` at the call site). (A/strong, verified)
- [[tigerbeetle-blog-four-fuzzers#^f5]] [zig/test] Count valid and invalid cases and assert both occur, so the test keeps testing what it claims. (A/strong, verified)
- [[tigerbeetle-blog-four-fuzzers#^f6]] [zig/test] Random seeds in tests, generated outside the process: Zig's `std.testing.random_seed` is passed on the CLI, so a crashing test still prints a replayable command. (A/strong, verified)
- [[tigerbeetle-blog-four-fuzzers#^f7]] [zig/test] Statistical asserts: run once with a fixed seed (92) to check counts, and again with the random seed for coverage. (A/strong, verified)
- [[tigerbeetle-blog-four-fuzzers#^f8]] [zig/test] [toolchain] At the time Zig's built-in fuzzing (`std.testing.fuzz`) did not work for the author. (A/strong, verified)
- [[tigerbeetle-blog-four-fuzzers#^f9]] [zig/test] Keep each CI run fast; total fuzzing time accumulates over runs. (A/strong, verified)
- [[tigerbeetle-blog-four-fuzzers#^f10]] [zig/test] Quick coverage check: put `unreachable` in a branch and see if the fuzzer crashes. (A/strong, verified)
- [[tigerbeetle-blog-four-fuzzers#^f11]] [zig/test] Purpose: find bugs in your understanding, not only in code. (A/strong, verified)
- [[tigerbeetle-blog-no-dynamic-memory#^f4]] [zig/memory] [zig/test] Wrong sizing is caught by assertions plus fuzzing rather than by resource exhaustion in production. (B/fair, verified)
- [[tigerbeetle-blog-sim-liveness#^f1]] [zig/test] Uniform random fault injection checks safety but hides liveness bugs, because every fault eventually heals. (B/fair, verified)
- [[tigerbeetle-blog-sim-liveness#^f2]] [zig/test] Liveness mode: heal a quorum core, make all other faults permanent, then require the core to finish all work within a timeout. (B/fair, verified)
- [[tigerbeetle-blog-sim-liveness#^f3]] [zig/test] It found a round-robin repair schedule that resonated with which replica held which entry, livelocking repair. (B/fair, verified)
- [[tigerbeetle-blog-snapshot-testing#^f1]] [zig/test] Why snapshots: they force readable text dumps of domain objects, keep tests from freezing the code, and focus tests on data in and out. (A/strong, verified)
- [[tigerbeetle-blog-snapshot-testing#^f2]] [zig/test] [zig/api] The primitive is a value, not a macro: a string literal plus its `SourceLocation`, passed to ordinary check functions. (A/strong, verified)
- [[tigerbeetle-blog-snapshot-testing#^f3]] [zig/test] The caller writes `snap(@src(), \\...)`, which Zig makes cheap. (A/strong, verified)
- [[tigerbeetle-blog-snapshot-testing#^f4]] [zig/test] Tests become `check(input, snap(...))`: a helper that serialises the result and diffs it. (A/strong, verified)
- [[tigerbeetle-blog-snapshot-testing#^f5]] [zig/test] Update on opt-in (env var or `.update()`), and touch files only when the test would fail anyway. (A/strong, verified)
- [[tigerbeetle-blog-snapshot-testing#^f6]] [zig/test] Windows `\r\n` breaks comparison; fix with `.gitattributes`. (A/strong, verified)
- [[tigerbeetle-blog-snapshot-testing#^f7]] [zig/test] Volatile fields are skipped with `<snap:ignore>`. (A/strong, verified)
- [[tigerbeetle-blog-snapshot-testing#^f8]] [zig/test] A useful library fits in under 500 lines. (A/strong, verified)

- [[tigerbeetle-blog-swarm-testing#^f2]] [zig/test] [zig/comptime] `std.meta.DeclEnum(Queue)` turns the public API into an enum; switching on it makes the compiler demand a test arm for every new public method. (A/strong, verified)

- [[zig-std-testing#^f17]] [zig/test] `expectEqualDeep` compares through pointers and fields; `expectEqualSlices` and `expectEqualStrings` print a diff-style report. `std/testing.zig:761` (A/strong, verified)
- [[zig-std-testing#^f18]] [zig/test] `testing.Reader` is an Io.Reader double that yields a scripted list of chunks, to test short-read handling. `std/testing.zig:1265` (A/strong, verified)
- [[zig-std-testing#^f19]] [zig/test] Test failures are errors (`error.TestExpectedEqual`, `TestUnexpectedResult`), so tests are `!void` and `try` every expect. `std/testing.zig:583` (A/strong, verified)
- [[zig-talk-async-io-zigtoberfest#^f12]] [zig/test] Planned: a testing Io with deterministic, seed-driven scheduling for fuzzing concurrency. @41:45 (A/strong, verified)
- [[zig-talk-kelley-flush#^f6]] [zig/api] [zig/test] Further benefits listed: freestanding reuse, leak checking for file descriptors and sockets, race testing at the Io layer, faking Io for unit tests, custom scheduling, cancellation in userland. @14:26 (A/strong, verified)
- [[zig-talk-kelley-tagged-union#^f3]] [zig/test] Bug-elimination ladder: what developers hit, what users report, full machine-code branch coverage, fuzzing, then LLM-found bugs. @30:16 (A/fair, verified)
- [[zig-talk-kelley-tagged-union#^f4]] [zig/test] With full coverage and fuzzing done well you skip waiting for user reports; non-trivial enhancements send you back to the start. @32:48 (A/fair, verified)
- [[zig-talk-roadmap-2026#^f14]] [zig/test] Integrated fuzzing: one fuzz test can replace many unit tests; run fuzz tests in a release mode for more iterations. Experimental in mid 2025. @1:24:57 (A/strong, verified)
- [[ziggit-choosing-allocator#^f7]] [zig/test] `FailingAllocator` tests OOM-handling paths; `testing.allocator` for normal tests. (B/fair, verified)
- [[ziggit-errdefer-double-free#^f3]] [zig/test] `std.testing.checkAllAllocationFailures` reruns a test failing each allocation in turn; with `testing.allocator` it also catches double frees. (B/strong, verified)
- [[ziggit-errdefer-double-free#^f4]] [zig/test] It requires a deterministic allocation count and reports swallowed OOM as `error.SwallowedOutOfMemoryError`. (B/strong, verified)
- [[ziggit-errdefer-double-free#^f5]] [zig/test] Limit: it only injects `OutOfMemory`, not other errors. (B/strong, verified)
- [[zls-repo#^f9]] [zig/test] [zig/memory] A build option turns on a FailingAllocator that fails 1 in N allocations at random in the real binary, to exercise OOM paths beyond unit tests. `src/testing.zig` (A/fair, verified)
- [[zls-repo#^f30]] [zig/test] Tests live in a separate tests/ tree (tests.zig imports each file in a comptime block) plus inline tests in src/ run as a second test artifact. `tests/tests.zig`, `build.zig` (A/fair, verified)
- [[zls-repo#^f31]] [zig/test] Feature tests are table-like: source text with a `<cursor>` marker and the expected output as multiline string literals, through a shared helper. `tests/lsp_features/hover.zig` (A/fair, verified)
- [[zls-repo#^f32]] [zig/test] Analysis tests are annotated Zig files: a comment with carets under a symbol states its expected type and value; build.zig adds one run step per file. `tests/analysis/array.zig`, `build.zig` (A/fair, verified)
- [[zls-repo#^f33]] [zig/test] Own expectEqual serialises both sides to JSON and prints a line diff, for readable failures on big structs. `src/testing.zig` (A/fair, verified)
- [[zls-repo#^f34]] [zig/test] Tests use `std.testing.allocator` as a file-level const, so leaks fail tests. `tests/lsp_features/hover.zig` (A/fair, verified)
